FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RC-CFS0ADMN/903/2025 Dated 12-11-2025
regarding altering the limits of Madapura village of Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk and Tumakuru
District and constituting a new revenue village called Madapura Thandya was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the
Karnataka Gazette Dated 17-11-2025 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to
be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated 17-11-2025.
And whereas, objections or suggestions have not been received by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here by makes the following
notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the table shall be
declared as habitation area of Madapura Thandya village of Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk of
Tumakuru District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of Madapura
Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Madapura Thandya of Madapura village
in Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk of Tumakuru District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above with survey

converted as habitation area (Gramatana) of Madapura Thandya hamlet of Madapura village in
Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk of Tumakuru District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as the State Government
may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964).

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RC –CFS0ADMN/637/2025 Dated:
18-09-2025 regarding altering the limits of KONDAGULI village of DEVAR
HIPPARAGI Taluk in Vijayapura District and constituting a new revenue sub
village called as MADIVALESHWARA NAGAR sub village was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated: 09-10-2025 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner Vijayapura district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 11-10-2025. And whereas, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of MADIVALESHWARA NAGAR village
of DEVAR HIPPARAGI Taluk in Vijayapura District convert the same to form
hamlet with in the boundary of KONDAGULI revenue village and same will here in
after be named as called MADIVALESHWARA NAGAR of KONDAGULI village
in DEVAR HIPPARAGI Taluk of Vijayapura District.

along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

REVISED FINAL NOTIFICATION
In the exercised of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue act 1964(Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to( iv)of rule 3 of the Karnataka land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience,the government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Revised final notification to alter the limits of Gundur
village of Hangal taluka,Haveri district and to constitute a new village called
Shivajipur village as specified in the schedule.
The revenue villages of Shivajipur from Gundur village in Hanagal taluka of
Haveri district have been declared as revenue villages by the government’s final
notification number;RD24LRD92dated;12-06-1995. whereas the gramathana area not
been demarcated and survey numbers which had been assigned to new villages were
not in order in the said final notification. the Whereas the draft of the Revised
preliminary Notification No.RC-CFSADMN/893/2025 Dated:14-11-2025 regarding
altering the limits of Gundur village of Hangal Taluk and Haveri District and
constituting a new revenue village called Shivajipur published as required by section
6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in

persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Haveri district.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:19-
11-2025And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following revisednotification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

village Gundur of Hangal Taluk and Haveri District to form a new revenue village
called as Shivajipur of Hangal taluk in Haveri District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RC-CFS/0/ADMN/261/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1858226 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560001
Date:31-12-2025

REVISED FINAL NOTIFICATION
In the exercised of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue act 1964(Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to( iv)of rule 3 of the Karnataka land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience,the Government of
Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential area by adding an area occupied
by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of Hirur village of Hangal Taluk of
Haveri District to convert the existing Hanumanakoppa unrecorded habitation in to a
hamlet/extention part of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said
hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule below.
The revenue villages namedHanumanakoppa from Hirur village in Hanagal
taluka of Haveri district has been declared as revenue villages by the government’s
final notification number RD11LRD95 dated;25-07-1997.and sinec an undocumented
settlement cannot be designated as a village station, A notification is hereby issued
under Section 94-D of the Karnataka Land revenue Act, 1964 declaring the land
having the survey numbers and extent specified in the Schedule as a small
settlement/extent/part of the unregistered settlement Hanumanakoppa revenue village.
Whereas the draft of the Notification No.RC-CFS0ADMN/261/2025,
Dated: 13-11-2025 regarding altering the limits of Hirur village of Hanagal Taluk in
Haveri District and constituting a new revenue sub village called Hanumanakoppa
sub village was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated: 17-11-2025
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 18-11-2025. And whereas, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as gramatana area of Hanumankoppa of Hangal taluk
ofHaveri District and convert the same to form hamlet extention with in the boundary
of Hanumankoppa Revenue village and same will here in after be named as
Hanumankoppa of Hangal taluk ofHaveri District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RC/32/2023-TAH2-CFS, Dated: 07-
03-2024 regarding altering the limits of Maskal village of Hiriyuru Taluk and
Chitradurga District and constituting a new revenue village called Mallenu,
K.R.Pura, Chithradevarahatti was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette Dated: 15.06.2024 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons
likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the
Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Chitradurga district.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 15.06.2024; And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
Maskal village of Hiriyuru Taluk Chitradurga District to form a new revenue
village called as Mallenu village of Hiriyuru Taluk Chitradurga District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

No: RC-CFS0ADMN/461/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1890234 Multi Storied Building
Banguru-560001
Date – 31.12.2025

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No.: RC-CFS0ADMN/461/2025
Dated 06.09.2025 regarding altering the limits of Maskal village of Hiriyuru Taluk
and Chitradurga District and constituting a new revenue village called Maskal Matti
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated 13.11.2025 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty
days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 13.11.2025; And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Maskal Matti village of Hiriyuru Taluk
of Chitradurga District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the
boundary of Maskal Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Maskal
Matti of Maskal village in Hiriyuru Taluk of Chitradurga District.

hamlet of Maskal village in Hiriyuru Taluk of Chitradurga District.
The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as the State
Government may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964).

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here by makes the following
notification, namely:-

District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of Banasandra Revenue

REVISED FINAL NOTIFICATION
In the exercised of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue act 1964(Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to( iv)of rule 3 of the Karnataka land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience,the government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Revised final notification to alter the limits of
Hirekanagi village of Hangal taluka,Haveri district and to constitute a new village
called shivajinagar and basavanagar village as specified in the schedule.
The revenue villages of shivajinagar and basavanagar from Hirekanagi
village in Hanagal taluka of Haveri district have been declared as revenue villages by
the government’s final notification number ;RD 69 BHUDAPA 2001 dated; 12-12-
2003.whereas the gramathana area not been demarcated and survey numbers which
had been assigned to new villages were not in order in the said final notification. the
Whereas the draft of the Revised preliminary Notification No.RC-
CFSADMN/942/2025 Dated:24-11-2025 regarding altering the limits of Hirekanagi
village of Hangal Taluk and Haveri District and constituting a new revenue village
called shivajinagar and basavanagar published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette, Dated: 25-11-2025 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons
likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the
Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Haveri district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:26-
11-2025 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following revised notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded frm the limits of the said
village Hirekanagi of Hangal Taluk and Haveri District to form a new revenue
village called as Shivajinagar of Hangal taluk in Haveri District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RC-CFS0ADMN/68/2024-RC Dated:
14-02-2024 regarding altering the limits of Gani village of Nidagundi Taluk and
Vijayapur District and constituting a new revenue village called Krishnateera Nagar
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated: 29-06-2024 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner Vijayapur district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 02-07-2024; And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Gani of Nidagundi Taluk and Vijayapur District to form a new revenue
village called as Krishnateera Nagar of Nidagundi Taluk in Vijayapur District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

and Belagavi District and constituting a new revenue village called Shivanagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated: 15-03-2025 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner Belagavi district.

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka

here by makes the following notification, namely:-

village Muragod of Saundatthi taluk in Belagavi District to form a new revenue
village called as Shivanagar of Saundatthi taluk Belagavi District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RC-CFS/129/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1371115 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560001
Date:31-12-2025.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RC-CFS/129/2024-RC
Dated: 18-04-2024 regarding altering the limits of Kannal village of Vijayapur Taluk
and Vijayapur District and constituting a new revenue village called Shantinagar K
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated:02-07-2024 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner Vijayapur district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 02-07-2024 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Kannal of Vijayapur Taluk and Vijayapur District to form a new revenue
village called as Shantinagar K of Vijayapur Taluk and Vijayapur District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
No.:RC-CFS0ADMN/824/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1921078 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560001
Date:31-12-2025

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No :RC-CFS0ADMN/824/2025 Dated:
11-11-2025 regarding altering the limits of Gajipur village of Hangal Taluk and
Haveri District and constituting a new revenue village called Hude was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated: 15-11-2025 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner Haveri district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 18-11-2025; And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Gajipur of Hangal Taluk and Haveri District to form a new revenue village
called as Hude of Hangal Taluk in Haveri District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.
